# はじめての日本語能力試験 N2単語 2500 — extracted reference tables

> Machine-extracted by `tools/extract_hajimete.py` (rasterise + macOS Vision OCR at 300 DPI) from:
> - `refs/Hajimete/はじめての日本語能力試験 N2単語 2500.pdf` (sha1 `1d7e6c24568a`, 314 pages, 48 MB)
> Regenerate rather than hand-edit.

## Trust rules — read before using a single line of this file

**1. This is OCR, not quotable as exact wording.** This book is a scanned image with no text layer (`pdffonts` prints an empty table), so every line below was read out of pixels. Errors cluster on kanji that carry furigana, because Vision merges the ruby stroke into the glyph under it — `雨→龍`, `下→辛`, `家→蒙`. Ruby-free prose on the same page comes out near-perfect. **Open the PDF before quoting any of this as the book's wording.**

**2. This is SECONDARY EVIDENCE.** A textbook corroborates **band, family and reading** — is this word in the N2 inventory, does this kanji have a second 訓読み, are these two words a same-shape pair. It **never sets a count or a length**: the 31 official sittings in `refs/JLPT_N2_NEW/` are the measuring stick for every number, and their `booklet.md`/`key.md` are exact where this file is not. Never derive a calibration number from inside a fence.

**3. Everything is fenced `[OCR ▼]` … `[OCR ▲]`** for exactly that reason — the same convention as `refs/JLPT_N2_NEW/*/script.md`. Text outside a fence is this repo's own annotation, not the book.

**Measured quality of this run:** 314 pages, 26089 lines kept, 1005 half-height ruby lines dropped, 819 page-columns split at a gutter, 0 printed underlines located.

Vision's own confidence on this run: mean 0.63, 38% of lines below 0.50. **Do not read that as an accuracy figure** — for Japanese, Vision quantises confidence coarsely (0.30 / 0.50 / 1.00) and plenty of 0.30 lines here are character-perfect while some 1.00 lines are not. It is a run-to-run comparator only; the real quality statement is section by section, in each section's note.

## Sections in this file

- **全編 — 見出し語 1〜2500(語義・例文・品詞)** — PDF pp.1–314

## 全編 — 見出し語 1〜2500(語義・例文・品詞)

**The N2 word list itself.** Each entry prints the headword, its reading in furigana, a part-of-speech tag, one example sentence, and EN/ZH/VI glosses. The Japanese is the usable half: Vision interleaves the three translation columns, so a gloss here is a hint and the example sentence is the evidence. Consumers: `question-authoring` (per-key verification, a third book beside Shin Kanzen and Soumatome) and `tools/lexical_profile.py` (reference corpus for the 読解 lexical-load gate).

### PDF page 3

[OCR ▼]
本ギ日語能力考試分級別系列え一，を以N2目的学者面制作。
本弔江集了日語能力考試中頻繁出現的、日常生活中也能用到的単司。每介章芦、每
个部分都有不同的主題，可通辻想像不同的景来学。配有英語、中文、越南語的対照
翻洋，可即吋商込単司、例句的意思。本形除N2 級別的基本単同以外。加上“同必詞”、“反
必司”、“美取司・炎文詞”を杉中的単詞及表、共收永了 2500司。
所有的汊字上都杯有假名、不用査字典就能自学也本的魅力所在。男附紅包卡片、
単詞及例句的音頻，每个章芦述配有模抄試題。
本ギ内容不但通用身在日本的学者、対在海外的学者来説也同祥明了易憧。遂
本単司不似是一本考武対策。対日語学者来説是能止日語水平更上一个台的不
可多得的参考弔。我佰衷心期待大家的好成績。
2016年10月
全体著者
Day la mot quyen thuoc bo sach durgc chia thco caip do cia Ky thi Nang lurc Nhat ngir va
quyen sach nay danh cho cac ban dang hurong din muc tieu thi dau N2
Ching toi da loc ra danh saich cac tir vug thurong xuat hien trong Ky thi Nang luc Nhat ngir
va ciing co ich trong sinh hoat thurong ngay. Co de tai theo tirng churong, tinng phan, de cac ban
co the vira hinh dumg cac tinh huong via hoc. Vi co kem theo ban dich tieng Anh, tieng Trung
Quoc va tieng Viet nen cac ban co the kiem tra y nghia cia tir ving va cau vi du mot cach de
dang. Co khoang 2.500 tir bao gom phin “Tir dong nghia'， “Tir trai nghia'， “Tir lien quan -Tir
tong drong” cia cac ti vrng can ban cap do N2 va tir vung, miu cau duge dua na d Goc kien
thtrc.
Tat ca cac chir Kanji diu co phien am cach doc nen ban co the hoc ma khong can den tr
dien cing la diem hap din cia cudn sach nay.
Ngoai ra, ching toi cing da chuan bi ca tim bia do, phan phat am ti vrng va cau vi du, bai
thi thtr trong tirng churong.
Day la bo sach co ngi dung ma durong nhien cac ban dang hoc tap tai Nhat va ca cac ban
dang hoc tai cac nroc ngoai Nhat Ban deu co the hinh dung de ding. Sa tay tir vug nay khong
chi la doi sach luyen thi ma con la mot quyen sach ho trg tinh than manh me cho cac ban hoc tap
uieng Nhat Chiing toi thanh tam chiic cac ban thi dau.
Thang 10 nam 2016
